# Skills · B · part 88

903 entries, brainrotify to brainstorm. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **brainrotify**: Rewrite developer-facing prose into playful Gen-Z "brainrot" language without changing executable code. Use when an AI coding agent or assistant needs to transform comments, docstrings, Markdown, README files, or other documentation text surfaces in one file or a directory while preserving syntax, logic, formatting, and code fences. Support optional backup creation and three intensity levels: `L`, `Mid`, and `W`. ([tengfone/agents](https://github.com/tengfone/agents/tree/HEAD/skills/brainrotify))

([MaikoCode/brainrotshorts-agent-skills](https://github.com/MaikoCode/brainrotshorts-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/brainrotshorts))

- **brains**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "run the brains pipeline", "start the brains workflow", "plan and implement from scratch", "do an ADR", "start with brainstorming", or invokes "/brains:brains". - **brainstorm**: Brainstorm [topic] [--target P] [--rounds N=2] [--mid-pct P=20] [--lenses generic|optimization] [--cross-model] [--fable] [--out FILE] [+Nk]. Multi-agent fan-out: ideate across diverse lenses (mixed STRONG/MID model tiers, mixed effort) → adversarially refute → debate → synthesize a ranked report.
